\newcommand{\metricbox}[3]{%

      \begin{minipage}{3.4cm}\centering
        {\color{txtsub}\scriptsize #1}\\[2pt]
        {\color{#3}\Large\bfseries #2}
      \end{minipage}
    };
  \end{tikzpicture}%
}
